| Should sanitized DOI and Egypt SSI exports replace the raw snapshots before they enter version control? | The [DOI guide](../../knowledge/evidence/sources/sha256/f723cc9719dcb14240a8b0af86bdc26342d42368ed48d4ca5a16dfb31b825ed0.md#L1986) and [Egypt design](../../knowledge/evidence/sources/sha256/765d5de5c9a3008124b334f133524ea636587d2f948e604c8aef4d24e307a406.md#L15) contain credential-bearing sections excluded from checkpoint 100 views. | Does “trade original id — by pass” mean bypass validation or exclude the field from Quick Search, and what should users see? | The [Quick Search notes](../../knowledge/evidence/sources/sha256/e6722f2a45316c6889bf3b94a5ac38f0ffaeac95ee40b3aa5f8134a25525362d.md#L5) give no expected result. | Pending; owner unknown |
| Should Cashflow Sub State be added to Quick Search, and which state and filter behavior should apply? | The [Quick Search notes](../../knowledge/evidence/sources/sha256/e6722f2a45316c6889bf3b94a5ac38f0ffaeac95ee40b3aa5f8134a25525362d.md#L49) say to confirm with a user but record no answer. | Pending; owner unknown |
| What date and state does “Right Top Value Today” filter, and what should toggling it off clear? | The [Quick Search notes](../../knowledge/evidence/sources/sha256/e6722f2a45316c6889bf3b94a5ac38f0ffaeac95ee40b3aa5f8134a25525362d.md#L50) do not define the query. | Pending; owner unknown |
| What should stamping do when zero or multiple RFI Nostro records match? | The [parent requirement](../../knowledge/evidence/sources/sha256/c3f430a1472df88805901bb83d0193abacfc7513b7f08cd98634c41cd9cc3954.md#L31) uses default processing for zero and a missing-Nostro exception for multiple. The [design Q&A](../../knowledge/evidence/sources/sha256/40e22b2943cc547fc99e2161cfc679967ff2b4701bb3bd753838a30060427be9.md#L86) raises a missing exception for zero and a multiple exception for multiple. | Pending; owner unknown |
| Do RFI portfolio changes always cause an economic amendment? | The [parent requirement](../../knowledge/evidence/sources/sha256/c3f430a1472df88805901bb83d0193abacfc7513b7f08cd98634c41cd9cc3954.md#L13) makes each RFI/non-RFI change economic. The [design Q&A](../../knowledge/evidence/sources/sha256/40e22b2943cc547fc99e2161cfc679967ff2b4701bb3bd753838a30060427be9.md#L91) asks whether cashflow state restricts this rule. | Pending; owner unknown |
| Which RFI stamping entry points are in scope? | The [parent requirement](../../knowledge/evidence/sources/sha256/c3f430a1472df88805901bb83d0193abacfc7513b7f08cd98634c41cd9cc3954.md#L18) scopes cashflow SSI and separates trade. The [design Q&A](../../knowledge/evidence/sources/sha256/40e22b2943cc547fc99e2161cfc679967ff2b4701bb3bd753838a30060427be9.md#L83) lists five entry points and leaves some open. | Pending; owner unknown |
| May adhoc SSI select an RFI Nostro for a non-RFI portfolio? | The [design Q&A](../../knowledge/evidence/sources/sha256/40e22b2943cc547fc99e2161cfc679967ff2b4701bb3bd753838a30060427be9.md#L88) asks for confirmation. The [UAT case](../../knowledge/evidence/sources/sha256/49f4ad86cd62da32d8d88657906d8777d67153cf089897146a4794851c4f0831.md#L10) allows this selection. | Pending; owner unknown |
| Does Notice to Receive control MT210 generation for non-RFI cashflows? | The [UAT cases](../../knowledge/evidence/sources/sha256/49f4ad86cd62da32d8d88657906d8777d67153cf089897146a4794851c4f0831.md#L8) report no SWIFT for `N` and generation for `Y`. The [parent requirement](../../knowledge/evidence/sources/sha256/c3f430a1472df88805901bb83d0193abacfc7513b7f08cd98634c41cd9cc3954.md#L36) scopes field 25 to KRW only. | Pending; owner unknown |
| Which SSI ID and Reference ID behavior is approved after manual selection and edits? | The [TO-BE requirement](../../knowledge/evidence/sources/sha256/cc76a535a53714bd911822576dd1152d2560395104805f0527a7271b39e2d9b0.md#L28) defines field-level behavior. The [UAT page](../../knowledge/evidence/sources/sha256/53c9e9db84d0715b1460e4ab617d118b3d178a79f8933afa2991b06f40f9f445.md) marks four Reference ID cases for retest. | Pending; owner unknown |
| What SSI refresh behavior applies after each exception and insert, update, or delete event? | The [refresh page](../../knowledge/evidence/sources/sha256/d047dd67137c6a2cfa96c818d42ad9801e0716e6e7242e1ff71ec2efc6f43f5d.md) gives labels but links only to missing flow images. | Pending; owner unknown |
| Which architecture option is the approved onboarding target: multiple blotters, one aggregate form, DB or configuration service, event or Kafka distribution, or Excel upload? Does the goal of avoiding per-entity production code changes remain current? | The [requirement](../../knowledge/evidence/sources/sha256/9efe5eda704afc9ed9860ee9d2b459f8c9e0a2eb67641a1d8302a37211b925a0.md#L14) selects a new blotter. The [design](../../knowledge/evidence/sources/sha256/2271f4dca3928dc2c6a8d4839de9f295ec5cae1f00275f42d5692863667cee82.md#L11) compares conflicting options and records no final approval. | Pending; owner unknown |
| Which configuration domains belong in onboarding, and which stay separate or backend-only, including Nostro, currency mapping and rounding, SWIFT, accounting, time zones, CPT, CFI, and precious-metal settings? | The [requirement](../../knowledge/evidence/sources/sha256/9efe5eda704afc9ed9860ee9d2b459f8c9e0a2eb67641a1d8302a37211b925a0.md#L22) and [static details](../../knowledge/evidence/sources/sha256/9efe5eda704afc9ed9860ee9d2b459f8c9e0a2eb67641a1d8302a37211b925a0.md#L36) include several domains. The [Q&A](../../knowledge/evidence/sources/sha256/35f121e944b7a609e9c2fdc8e81c2f65804e755fdf760433acda9c59ab1be930.md#L10) excludes moving Nostro into self-service, while the [technical design](../../knowledge/evidence/sources/sha256/2271f4dca3928dc2c6a8d4839de9f295ec5cae1f00275f42d5692863667cee82.md#L97) includes it in a design option. | Pending; owner unknown |
| Which test, stage sign-off, production maker/checker, CPT pilot, and cleanup steps remain required after users configure a new entity? | The [Q&A](../../knowledge/evidence/sources/sha256/35f121e944b7a609e9c2fdc8e81c2f65804e755fdf760433acda9c59ab1be930.md#L26) records a proposed rollout sequence but leaves its status blank. | Pending; owner unknown |
| Can users update or delete onboarding data after initial creation, can the booking FMID ever change, what approval controls apply, and how is historical data handled? | The [requirement](../../knowledge/evidence/sources/sha256/9efe5eda704afc9ed9860ee9d2b459f8c9e0a2eb67641a1d8302a37211b925a0.md#L27) asks how updates take effect. | Which user roles may create, read, and check changes; may one person act as both maker and checker; and which status transitions are authoritative? | The [profile requirement](../../knowledge/evidence/sources/sha256/9efe5eda704afc9ed9860ee9d2b459f8c9e0a2eb67641a1d8302a37211b925a0.md#L18) describes static-ops edit access. | What is the authoritative source for branch codes, and how should the two-character rule handle leading zeroes and invalid values? | The [requirement](../../knowledge/evidence/sources/sha256/9efe5eda704afc9ed9860ee9d2b459f8c9e0a2eb67641a1d8302a37211b925a0.md#L35) asks for a golden source and additional validation. | Does currency precision and rounding apply to FMRP and all cashflows or only the stated Murex use, how does ISO mapping relate to the entity form, and what is the no-config behavior? | The [requirement](../../knowledge/evidence/sources/sha256/9efe5eda704afc9ed9860ee9d2b459f8c9e0a2eb67641a1d8302a37211b925a0.md#L36) says no rounding configuration uses the upstream amount. | Which release-time and cutoff rules apply: local or GMT input, date offset, business-day unit, timezone source, and missing-config default? | The [requirement](../../knowledge/evidence/sources/sha256/9efe5eda704afc9ed9860ee9d2b459f8c9e0a2eb67641a1d8302a37211b925a0.md#L40) proposes VD-2, VD-1, or VD and asks about local-time conversion. | Which SWIFT BIC rules are current for sender-country derivation, field 53 fallback and currency uniqueness, and field 58 requirements? | The [requirement](../../knowledge/evidence/sources/sha256/9efe5eda704afc9ed9860ee9d2b459f8c9e0a2eb67641a1d8302a37211b925a0.md#L44) proposes those rules but records no approval. | Which accounting rules govern currency suppression, country code, RTN or RTO selection, debit and credit codes, and the zone that starts the accounting job? | The [requirement](../../knowledge/evidence/sources/sha256/9efe5eda704afc9ed9860ee9d2b459f8c9e0a2eb67641a1d8302a37211b925a0.md#L49) leaves some display and country questions open. | For each onboarding field, should users enter the value or should the system obtain it from a golden source or another system? | The [FAQ](../../knowledge/evidence/sources/sha256/35f121e944b7a609e9c2fdc8e81c2f65804e755fdf760433acda9c59ab1be930.md#L20) repeats this question for several settings, but the linked screenshots are not retained. | Pending; owner unknown |
| Which Murex settlement value-date window is current for eligible entities: seven system business days with T-1 past-date handling, or the earlier nine-day window? | The [H2 DOI](../../knowledge/evidence/sources/sha256/e94ab3d36db4dd99e021cf7328309f2882dfb9a6f61f0379395d501e809c2789.md#L178) states seven system business days and T-1 for past dates. These scopes do not resolve the current eligible-entity rule. | Pending; owner unknown |
